# Skills · A · part 68

897 entries, afromt-eval to agb-mitwirkungspflichten-leistungsstoerung. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **afromt-eval**: This benchmark evaluates machine translation capabilities across eight morphologically rich African languages translated from English. It specifically probes how well models handle complex morphosyntactic features like noun classification and verb extensions in low-resource settings. Use when the user wants to benchmark on AFROMT, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports BLEU. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/afromt-eval) · ★3)

- **afrus-plan**: Stage 1 of the afrus issue workflow. Reads a GitHub issue exhaustively, analyzes the affected code, optionally queries production read-only for context, and produces an OpenSpec change proposal with a TDD-ordered task list. Stops for approval before any code is written. Use when the user gives an issue number or URL, says "plan issue N", "analiza el issue N", or invokes /afrus-plan. Requires a multi-repo workspace with a .claude/repos.json registry. ([duendecillo2/skills](https://github.com/duendecillo2/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/afrus/afrus-plan) · ★1)
- **afrus-pr**: Stage 4 of the afrus issue workflow. Re-runs the verification gates, runs code review agents, then asks permission before pushing and opens PRs against the correct base branch with the team's structured comment format. Use when the user says "hace el PR", "abri el pull request", or invokes /afrus-pr. Never pushes or opens a PR without explicit approval. Only applies inside the multi-repo workspace with a .claude/repos.json registry. ([duendecillo2/skills](https://github.com/duendecillo2/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/afrus/afrus-pr) · ★1)
- **afrus-verify**: Stage 3 of the afrus issue workflow. Use after implementing a change, or when the user says "verifica", "corre los tests", or invokes /afrus-verify. Requires a multi-repo workspace with a .claude/repos.json registry. ([duendecillo2/skills](https://github.com/duendecillo2/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/afrus/afrus-verify) · ★1)
